It's only natural that she left.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Emphasizing something that is just or necessary."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She's only gone and run off with the milkman!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to express surprise or consternation at an action."],"tags":["UK","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Sure you're only gorgeous!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Just, simply, undoubtedly."],"tags":["Ireland","informal","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They rallied from a three-goal deficit only to lose in the final two minutes of play.","type":"example"},{"text":"I spent years studying a degree only to learn there was no requirement for such a certification.","type":"example"},{"text":"I helped him out only for him to betray me.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Introduces a disappointing or surprising outcome that renders futile something previously mentioned."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"glosses":["Above all others; particularly."],"tags":["not-comparable","obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈoʊn.li/"},{"ipa":"/ˈəʊn.li/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈəʊn.i/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈaːn.li/","tags":["Northumbria"]},{"ipa":"/ˈoʊn.li/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈoːn.li]","tags":["Canada"]},{"ipa":"[ˈɔːn.lɪ]","tags":["Canada","dialectal"]},{"ipa":"/ˈonli/","tags":["South-Asia"]},{"ipa":"/(w)ɵnˈli/","tags":["South-Asia","dialectal"]},{"ipa":"/ˈwənli/","tags":["South-Asia"]},{"ipa":"/oːnlɪ/","tags":["Early","Modern"]},{"ipa":"/wɔnlɪ/","tags":["Early","Modern","rare"]}],"upstream_line":183052,"upstream_offset":873493805,"upstream_record_sha256":"9e971dd6df2810dbdcef3e9dba1dc658c5140ee8da129dd03abac65d37c274cf","word":"only"}
